FROM (
    SELECT TOP(1) [u].[UserId], [u].[BranchId], [u].[CreatedOn], [u].[DesignationId], [u].[Email], [u].[EmployeeId], [u].[IsActive], [u].[ModifiedOn], [u].[Password], [u].[PhoneNumber], [u].[UserName]
    FROM [Users] AS [u]
    WHERE [u].[EmployeeId] = @employeeId
) AS [u1]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [u0].[UserRoleId], [u0].[RoleId], [u0].[UserId], [r].[RoleId] AS [RoleId0], [r].[RoleName]
    FROM [UserRoles] AS [u0]
    INNER JOIN [Roles] AS [r] ON [u0].[RoleId] = [r].[RoleId]
) AS [s] ON [u1].[UserId] = [s].[UserId]
ORDER BY [u1].[UserId], [s].[UserRoleId]

    WHERE [a].[AccountNumber] = @accountNumber
) AS [s]
LEFT JOIN [Collaterals] AS [c] ON [s].[AccountId] = [c].[AccountId]
ORDER BY [s].[AccountId], [s].[BranchSettingId0]
